```turtle
@prefix nltl: <https://w3id.org/nltl/vocab#> .
@prefix sh: <http://www.w3.org/ns/shacl#> .
@prefix xsd: <http://www.w3.org/2001/XMLSchema#> .

<urn:nltl:generated-shape:I2-062-ShipShape>
    a sh:NodeShape ;
    sh:targetClass nltl:ship ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:directCalculationUsed ;
        sh:datatype xsd:boolean ;
        sh:hasValue true ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:maxCount 1 ;
        sh:message "The ship must record that direct calculations are used."
    ] ;
    sh:sparql [
        a sh:SPARQLConstraint ;
        sh:message "Each linked load carrying stringer or web frame must form part of a grillage system." ;
        sh:select """
            PREFIX nltl: <https://w3id.org/nltl/vocab#>
            PREFIX sh: <http://www.w3.org/ns/shacl#>

            SELECT $this
            WHERE {
                $this nltl:hasStructuralMember ?member .
                ?member a ?memberType .
                FILTER (
                    ?memberType IN (
                        nltl:loadCarryingStringer,
                        nltl:webFrame
                    )
                )
                FILTER NOT EXISTS {
                    ?member nltl:partOfGrillageSystem ?grillage .
                    ?grillage a nltl:grillageSystem .
                }
            }
        """
    ] .
```

```turtle
    ] ;

        a sh:SPARQLConstraint ;
        sh:message "The ship must record that direct calculation was used when it has a load carrying stringer or web frame forming part of a grillage system." ;

            PREFIX xsd: <http://www.w3.org/2001/XMLSchema#>

            SELECT $this

                $this nltl:hasStructuralMember ?member .

                    ?memberType IN (

                        nltl:webFrame
                    )
                )
                FILTER NOT EXISTS {
                    $this nltl:directCalculationUsed ?used .
                    FILTER (?used = true)
                }

        """
```

REQUIREMENT 152: IMO-097
====================================================================================================

A. STABLE IDENTITY AND SOURCE EVIDENCE
Audit order: 152
Batch: BATCH_16
Requirement ID: IMO-097
Source family: IMO
Verification mode: DIRECT_STATIC
Source document: International Code for Ships Operating in Polar Waters (Polar Code)
Source revision: Resolution MSC.385(94), adopted 21 Nov 2014; consolidated with MEPC.264(68), adopted 15 May 2015
Clause / section / page: 10.3.2.1 / Part I-A Chapter 10 - Survival craft communications / 29
Source URL: https://wwwcdn.imo.org/localresources/en/KnowledgeCentre/IndexofIMOResolutions/MSCResolutions/MSC.385%2894%29.pdf
Local source PDF: MVP/RELEVANT FILES/MSC.385(94).pdf
Local source PDF SHA-256: 6c6a038bff68ee906dd97c41e95fb4442917c014c8e79228f15e80cc5afa5c1f
Locked context path: MVP/SHACL_GENERATION_PIPELINE/experiments/FULL_REPAIR_V2/RUN_01/runs/RUN-IMO-097-20260915T112117217172Z/artifacts/context_pack_initial.json
Original source text:
For ships intended to operate in low air temperature, all rescue boats and lifeboats, whenever released for evacuation, shall: for distress alerting, carry one device for transmitting ship-to-shore alerts; in order to be located, carry one device for transmitting signals for location; and for on-scene communications, carry one device for transmitting and receiving on-scene communications.
Normalized requirement:
IF ship_operates_in_low_air_temperature = true, THEN every released rescue boat and lifeboat shall carry at least one ship-to-shore distress-alert device, one location-signal device, and one on-scene transmit/receive communication device.
Required inputs: Operating-temperature class, craft inventory, and assigned device inventory

Local source PDF: MVP/RELEVANT FILES/MSC.385(94).pdf

Locked context path: MVP/SHACL_GENERATION_PIPELINE/experiments/FULL_REPAIR_V2/RUN_01/runs/RUN-IMO-103-20260915T112830395283Z/artifacts/context_pack_initial.json
Original source text:
The Administration may allow the use of a person(s) other than the master, chief mate or officers of the navigational watch to satisfy the requirements for training, provided that: this person(s) shall be qualified and certified in accordance with regulation II/2 of the STCW Convention and section A-II/2 of the STCW Code, and meet the advance training requirements set out in the table in paragraph 12.3.1; while operating in polar waters the ship carries a sufficient number of persons meeting the appropriate training requirements for polar waters to cover all watches; this person(s) is subject to the Administration's minimum hours of rest requirements at all times; when operating in waters other than open waters or bergy waters, the master, chief mate and officers in charge of a navigational watch on passenger ships and tankers shall meet the applicable basic training requirements set out in the table in paragraph 12.3.1; and when operating in waters with an ice concentration of more than 2/10, the master, chief mate and officers in charge of a navigational watch on cargo ships other than tankers shall meet the applicable basic training requirements set out in the table in paragraph 12.3.1.
Normalized requirement:
IF alternate_qualified_person_used = true, THEN administration_permission_status = approved and all five conditions in clause 12.3.2 shall be satisfied, including STCW II/2 and A-II/2 certification, advanced polar training, watch coverage, rest compliance, and the condition-specific basic-training safeguards for designated officers.
Required inputs: Administration permission, credentials, watch schedule, rest records, and operating ice condition

```turtle
@prefix gen: <urn:nltl:generated-shape:> .
@prefix nltl: <https://w3id.org/nltl/vocab#> .
@prefix qudt: <http://qudt.org/schema/qudt/> .
@prefix sh: <http://www.w3.org/ns/shacl#> .
@prefix unit: <http://qudt.org/vocab/unit/> .
@prefix xsd: <http://www.w3.org/2001/XMLSchema#> .

gen:IMO_103_ShipShape a sh:NodeShape ;
    sh:targetClass nltl:ship ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:alternateQualifiedPersonUsed ;
        sh:datatype xsd:boolean ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:maxCount 1
    ] ;
    sh:or (
        [
            sh:property [
                sh:path nltl:alternateQualifiedPersonUsed ;
                sh:hasValue false
            ]
        ]
        [
            sh:property [
                sh:path nltl:alternateQualifiedPersonUsed ;
                sh:datatype xsd:boolean ;
                sh:hasValue true ;
                sh:minCount 1 ;
                sh:maxCount 1
            ] ;
            sh:property [
                sh:path nltl:administrationPermissionStatus ;
                sh:datatype xsd:boolean ;
                sh:hasValue true ;
                sh:minCount 1 ;
                sh:maxCount 1
            ] ;
            sh:property [
                sh:path nltl:stcwConventionRegulationIi2ComplianceStatus ;
                sh:datatype xsd:boolean ;
                sh:hasValue true ;
                sh:minCount 1 ;
                sh:maxCount 1
            ] ;
            sh:property [
                sh:path nltl:stcwCodeSectionAIi2ComplianceStatus ;
                sh:datatype xsd:boolean ;
                sh:hasValue true ;
                sh:minCount 1 ;
                sh:maxCount 1
            ] ;
            sh:property [
                sh:path nltl:advancedPolarTrainingStatus ;
                sh:datatype xsd:boolean ;
                sh:hasValue true ;
                sh:minCount 1 ;
                sh:maxCount 1
            ] ;
            sh:property [
                sh:path nltl:watchCoverageStatus ;
                sh:datatype xsd:boolean ;
                sh:hasValue true ;
                sh:minCount 1 ;
                sh:maxCount 1
            ] ;
            sh:property [
                sh:path nltl:restComplianceStatus ;
                sh:datatype xsd:boolean ;
                sh:hasValue true ;
                sh:minCount 1 ;
                sh:maxCount 1
            ] ;
            sh:or (
                gen:IMO_103_PassengerOrTankerCase
                gen:IMO_103_CargoCase
            )
        ]
    ) .

gen:IMO_103_PassengerOrTankerCase a sh:NodeShape ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:shipType ;
        sh:datatype xsd:string ;
        sh:in ( "passenger ship" "tanker" ) ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:maxCount 1
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:iceCondition ;
        sh:datatype xsd:string ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:maxCount 1
    ] ;
    sh:sparql [
        sh:message "For a passenger ship or tanker operating in waters other than open waters or bergy waters, designated officers must meet the applicable basic training requirements." ;
        sh:select """
            PREFIX nltl: <https://w3id.org/nltl/vocab#>
            PREFIX xsd: <http://www.w3.org/2001/XMLSchema#>
            SELECT $this
            WHERE {
                $this nltl:iceCondition ?condition .
                FILTER (
                    ?condition != "open waters"^^xsd:string &&
                    ?condition != "bergy waters"^^xsd:string
                )
                FILTER NOT EXISTS {
                    $this nltl:designatedOfficerBasicTrainingStatus true
                }
            }
        """
    ] .

gen:IMO_103_CargoCase a sh:NodeShape ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:shipType ;
        sh:datatype xsd:string ;
        sh:in ( "cargo ship other than tanker" ) ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:maxCount 1
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:iceConcentration ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:maxCount 1 ;
        sh:node [
            sh:class qudt:QuantityValue ;
            sh:property [
                sh:path qudt:numericValue ;
                sh:datatype xsd:decimal ;
                sh:minCount 1 ;
                sh:maxCount 1
            ] ;
            sh:property [
                sh:path qudt:unit ;
                sh:hasValue unit:UNITLESS ;
                sh:minCount 1 ;
                sh:maxCount 1
            ]
        ]
    ] ;
    sh:sparql [
        sh:message "For a cargo ship other than a tanker operating with an ice concentration greater than 2/10, designated officers must meet the applicable basic training requirements." ;
        sh:select """
            PREFIX nltl: <https://w3id.org/nltl/vocab#>
            PREFIX qudt: <http://qudt.org/schema/qudt/>
            PREFIX xsd: <http://www.w3.org/2001/XMLSchema#>
            SELECT $this
            WHERE {
                $this nltl:iceConcentration ?quantity .
                ?quantity qudt:numericValue ?concentration .
                FILTER (?concentration > "0.2"^^xsd:decimal)
                FILTER NOT EXISTS {
                    $this nltl:designatedOfficerBasicTrainingStatus true
                }
            }
        """
    ] .
```

```turtle
@prefix unit: <http://qudt.org/vocab/unit/> .
@prefix xsd: <http://www.w3.org/2001/XMLSchema#> .

gen:IMO_103_ShipShape
    a sh:NodeShape ;
    sh:targetClass nltl:ship ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:alternateQualifiedPersonUsed ;
        sh:datatype xsd:boolean ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:maxCount 1 ;
    ] ;
    sh:or (
        [ sh:node gen:IMO_103_AlternateNotUsedShape ]
        [ sh:node gen:IMO_103_AlternateUsedShape ]
    ) ;
    sh:message "The ship must specify whether an alternate qualified person is used and satisfy the applicable clause 12.3.2 conditions." .

gen:IMO_103_AlternateNotUsedShape
    a sh:NodeShape ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:alternateQualifiedPersonUsed ;
        sh:datatype xsd:boolean ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:maxCount 1 ;
        sh:hasValue false ;
    ] .

gen:IMO_103_AlternateUsedShape
    a sh:NodeShape ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:alternateQualifiedPersonUsed ;
        sh:datatype xsd:boolean ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:maxCount 1 ;
        sh:hasValue true ;
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:administrationPermissionStatus ;

        sh:maxCount 1 ;
        sh:hasValue true ;
    ] ;

        sh:hasValue true ;
    ] ;

        sh:path nltl:restComplianceStatus ;
        sh:datatype xsd:boolean ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:maxCount 1 ;
        sh:hasValue true ;
    ] ;
    sh:or (
        [ sh:node gen:IMO_103_PassengerTankerOpenOrBergyShape ]
        [ sh:node gen:IMO_103_PassengerTankerOtherWatersShape ]
        [ sh:node gen:IMO_103_CargoLowIceConcentrationShape ]
        [ sh:node gen:IMO_103_CargoHighIceConcentrationShape ]
    ) .

gen:IMO_103_PassengerTankerOpenOrBergyShape
    a sh:NodeShape ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:shipType ;
        sh:datatype xsd:string ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:maxCount 1 ;
        sh:in ( "passenger ship" "tanker" )
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:iceCondition ;
        sh:datatype xsd:string ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:maxCount 1 ;
        sh:in ( "open waters" "bergy waters" )
    ] .

gen:IMO_103_PassengerTankerOtherWatersShape
    a sh:NodeShape ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:shipType ;
        sh:datatype xsd:string ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:maxCount 1 ;
        sh:in ( "passenger ship" "tanker" )
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:iceCondition ;
        sh:datatype xsd:string ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:maxCount 1 ;
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:designatedOfficerBasicTrainingStatus ;
        sh:datatype xsd:boolean ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:maxCount 1 ;
        sh:hasValue true ;
    ] ;
    sh:sparql [
        sh:message "Passenger ships and tankers operating in waters other than open waters or bergy waters require designated-officer basic training." ;
        sh:select """
            PREFIX nltl: <https://w3id.org/nltl/vocab#>
            PREFIX xsd: <http://www.w3.org/2001/XMLSchema#>
            SELECT $this
            WHERE {
                $this nltl:iceCondition ?condition .
                FILTER (?condition NOT IN ("open waters"^^xsd:string, "bergy waters"^^xsd:string))
            }
        """
    ] .

gen:IMO_103_CargoLowIceConcentrationShape
    a sh:NodeShape ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:shipType ;
        sh:datatype xsd:string ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:maxCount 1 ;
        sh:hasValue "cargo ship other than tanker"
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:iceConcentration ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:maxCount 1 ;
        sh:node [
            sh:class qudt:QuantityValue ;
            sh:property [
                sh:path qudt:numericValue ;
                sh:datatype xsd:decimal ;
                sh:minCount 1 ;
                sh:maxCount 1 ;
                sh:maxInclusive "0.2"^^xsd:decimal ;
            ] ;
            sh:property [
                sh:path qudt:unit ;
                sh:nodeKind sh:IRI ;
                sh:minCount 1 ;
                sh:maxCount 1 ;
                sh:hasValue unit:UNITLESS ;
            ]
        ]
    ] .

gen:IMO_103_CargoHighIceConcentrationShape
    a sh:NodeShape ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:shipType ;
        sh:datatype xsd:string ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:maxCount 1 ;
        sh:hasValue "cargo ship other than tanker"
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:iceConcentration ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:maxCount 1 ;
        sh:node [
            sh:class qudt:QuantityValue ;
            sh:property [
                sh:path qudt:numericValue ;
                sh:datatype xsd:decimal ;
                sh:minCount 1 ;
                sh:maxCount 1 ;
                sh:minExclusive "0.2"^^xsd:decimal ;
            ] ;
            sh:property [
                sh:path qudt:unit ;
                sh:nodeKind sh:IRI ;
                sh:minCount 1 ;
                sh:maxCount 1 ;
                sh:hasValue unit:UNITLESS ;
            ]
        ]
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:designatedOfficerBasicTrainingStatus ;
        sh:datatype xsd:boolean ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:maxCount 1 ;
        sh:hasValue true ;
    ] .
```

CASE I2-004-G-P03
Original expected verdict: PASS
Original rationale: A single represented UIWL case is itself the maximum.
Source clause: I2.1.2.2
Test pattern: UNSPECIFIED
RDF path: MVP/SHACL_GENERATION_PIPELINE/evaluation/BEHAVIORAL_RDF_R13/rdf/I2/I2-004/I2-004-G-P03.ttl
RDF SHA-256: c6b990adc6c06c36995692a3abf0412c974bb29c8ad7c03562b6f2248a11bf22
Full unmodified RDF Turtle:
```turtle
@prefix ex: <https://w3id.org/nltl/benchmark/i2-batch-g/I2-004-P03/> .
@prefix nltl: <https://w3id.org/nltl/vocab#> .
@prefix qudt: <http://qudt.org/schema/qudt/> .
@prefix unit: <http://qudt.org/vocab/unit/> .
@prefix xsd: <http://www.w3.org/2001/XMLSchema#> .

ex:ship a nltl:ship ;
    nltl:hasUpperIceWaterlineCase ex:waterlineCase1 ;
    nltl:upperIceWaterlineDisplacement ex:selectedDui .

ex:caseDisp1 a qudt:QuantityValue ;
    qudt:numericValue 8.0 ;
    qudt:unit unit:KiloTON_Metric .

ex:selectedDui a qudt:QuantityValue ;
    qudt:numericValue 8.0 ;
    qudt:unit unit:KiloTON_Metric .

ex:waterlineCase1 a nltl:upperIceWaterlineCase ;
```

```turtle
@prefix gs: <urn:nltl:generated-shape:> .

@prefix xsd: <http://www.w3.org/2001/XMLSchema#> .

gs:I2_004_StaticShape a sh:NodeShape ;
    sh:targetClass nltl:ship ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:hasUpperIceWaterlineCase ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:node [
            sh:class nltl:upperIceWaterlineCase ;
            sh:property [
                sh:path nltl:waterlineCaseDisplacement ;
                sh:minCount 1 ;
                sh:maxCount 1 ;
                sh:node [
                    sh:class qudt:QuantityValue ;
                    sh:property [
                        sh:path qudt:numericValue ;
                        sh:minCount 1 ;
                        sh:maxCount 1 ;
                        sh:datatype xsd:decimal
                    ] ;
                    sh:property [
                        sh:path qudt:unit ;
                        sh:minCount 1 ;
                        sh:maxCount 1 ;
                        sh:hasValue unit:KiloTON_Metric
                    ]
                ]
            ]
        ]
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:upperIceWaterlineDisplacement ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:maxCount 1 ;
        sh:node [
            sh:class qudt:QuantityValue ;
            sh:property [
                sh:path qudt:numericValue ;
                sh:minCount 1 ;
                sh:maxCount 1 ;
                sh:datatype xsd:decimal
            ] ;
            sh:property [
                sh:path qudt:unit ;
                sh:minCount 1 ;
                sh:maxCount 1 ;
                sh:hasValue unit:KiloTON_Metric
            ]
        ]
    ] ;
    sh:sparql [
        sh:message "The upper ice waterline displacement must equal the greatest displacement among the linked upper ice waterline cases." ;
        sh:select """
            PREFIX nltl: <https://w3id.org/nltl/vocab#>
            PREFIX qudt: <http://qudt.org/schema/qudt/>
            PREFIX unit: <http://qudt.org/vocab/unit/>
            PREFIX xsd: <http://www.w3.org/2001/XMLSchema#>

            SELECT $this
            WHERE {
                $this nltl:upperIceWaterlineDisplacement ?duiQuantity .
                ?duiQuantity qudt:numericValue ?duiValue ;
                             qudt:unit unit:KiloTON_Metric .
                FILTER (datatype(?duiValue) = xsd:decimal)

                $this nltl:hasUpperIceWaterlineCase ?case .
                ?case nltl:waterlineCaseDisplacement ?caseQuantity .
                ?caseQuantity qudt:numericValue ?caseValue ;
                              qudt:unit unit:KiloTON_Metric .
                FILTER (datatype(?caseValue) = xsd:decimal)

                FILTER (
                    ?caseValue > ?duiValue ||
                    NOT EXISTS {
                        $this nltl:hasUpperIceWaterlineCase ?equalCase .
                        ?equalCase nltl:waterlineCaseDisplacement ?equalQuantity .
                        ?equalQuantity qudt:numericValue ?equalValue ;
                                       qudt:unit unit:KiloTON_Metric .
                        FILTER (datatype(?equalValue) = xsd:decimal)
                        FILTER (?equalValue = ?duiValue)
                    }
                )
            }
        """
    ] .
```

            ]

```turtle
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:hasUpperIceWaterlineCase ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:node [
            sh:class nltl:upperIceWaterlineCase ;
            sh:property [
                sh:path nltl:waterlineCaseDisplacement ;
                sh:minCount 1 ;
                sh:maxCount 1 ;
                sh:node [
                    sh:class qudt:QuantityValue ;
                    sh:property [
                        sh:path qudt:numericValue ;
                        sh:minCount 1 ;
                        sh:maxCount 1 ;
                        sh:datatype xsd:decimal
                    ] ;
                    sh:property [
                        sh:path qudt:unit ;
                        sh:minCount 1 ;
                        sh:maxCount 1 ;
                        sh:hasValue unit:KiloTON_Metric
                    ]
                ]
            ]
        ] ;
        sh:message "Each upper ice waterline case shall provide one displacement in kilotonnes."
    ] ;
    sh:sparql [
        a sh:SPARQLConstraint ;
        sh:message "The upper ice waterline displacement shall equal the greatest linked waterline case displacement, within a relative tolerance of 1e-6 and an absolute fallback of 1e-9 kt." ;
        sh:select """
            PREFIX nltl: <https://w3id.org/nltl/vocab#>
            PREFIX qudt: <http://qudt.org/schema/qudt/>
            PREFIX xsd: <http://www.w3.org/2001/XMLSchema#>

            SELECT $this ?duiValue ?caseValue
            WHERE {
                $this nltl:upperIceWaterlineDisplacement ?duiNode ;
                       nltl:hasUpperIceWaterlineCase ?caseNode .
                ?duiNode qudt:numericValue ?duiValue .
                ?caseNode nltl:waterlineCaseDisplacement ?caseDisplacementNode .
                ?caseDisplacementNode qudt:numericValue ?caseValue .

                FILTER NOT EXISTS {
                    $this nltl:hasUpperIceWaterlineCase ?greaterCaseNode .
                    ?greaterCaseNode nltl:waterlineCaseDisplacement ?greaterDisplacementNode .
                    ?greaterDisplacementNode qudt:numericValue ?greaterValue .
                    FILTER (?greaterValue > ?caseValue)
                }

                FILTER (
                    ABS(?duiValue - ?caseValue) >
                    (0.000001 * ABS(?caseValue) + 0.000000001)
                )
            }
        """
```

Full unmodified selected SHACL:
```turtle
@prefix gen: <urn:nltl:generated-shape:> .
@prefix nltl: <https://w3id.org/nltl/vocab#> .
@prefix qudt: <http://qudt.org/schema/qudt/> .
@prefix sh: <http://www.w3.org/ns/shacl#> .
@prefix unit: <http://qudt.org/vocab/unit/> .
@prefix xsd: <http://www.w3.org/2001/XMLSchema#> .

gen:I2_004_ShipShape
    a sh:NodeShape ;
    sh:targetClass nltl:ship ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:upperIceWaterlineDisplacement ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:maxCount 1 ;
        sh:node [
            sh:class qudt:QuantityValue ;
            sh:property [
                sh:path qudt:numericValue ;
                sh:datatype xsd:decimal ;
                sh:minCount 1 ;
                sh:maxCount 1
            ] ;
            sh:property [
                sh:path qudt:unit ;
                sh:hasValue unit:KiloTON_Metric ;
                sh:minCount 1 ;
                sh:maxCount 1
            ]
        ]
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:hasUpperIceWaterlineCase ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:node [
            sh:class nltl:upperIceWaterlineCase ;
            sh:property [
                sh:path nltl:waterlineCaseDisplacement ;
                sh:minCount 1 ;
                sh:maxCount 1 ;
                sh:node [
                    sh:class qudt:QuantityValue ;
                    sh:property [
                        sh:path qudt:numericValue ;
                        sh:datatype xsd:decimal ;
                        sh:minCount 1 ;
                        sh:maxCount 1
                    ] ;
                    sh:property [
                        sh:path qudt:unit ;
                        sh:hasValue unit:KiloTON_Metric ;
                        sh:minCount 1 ;
                        sh:maxCount 1
                    ]
                ]
            ]
        ]
    ] ;
    sh:sparql [
        a sh:SPARQLConstraint ;
        sh:message "The upper ice waterline displacement must equal a waterline case displacement and must not be less than any linked case displacement." ;
        sh:select """
            PREFIX nltl: <https://w3id.org/nltl/vocab#>
            PREFIX qudt: <http://qudt.org/schema/qudt/>
            PREFIX xsd: <http://www.w3.org/2001/XMLSchema#>

            SELECT $this
            WHERE {
                $this nltl:upperIceWaterlineDisplacement ?duiNode .
                ?duiNode qudt:numericValue ?duiValue .

                $this nltl:hasUpperIceWaterlineCase ?case .
                ?case nltl:waterlineCaseDisplacement ?caseDisplacement .
                ?caseDisplacement qudt:numericValue ?caseValue .
                FILTER (?caseValue = ?duiValue)

                FILTER NOT EXISTS {
                    $this nltl:hasUpperIceWaterlineCase ?otherCase .
                    ?otherCase nltl:waterlineCaseDisplacement ?otherDisplacement .
                    ?otherDisplacement qudt:numericValue ?otherValue .
                    FILTER (?otherValue > ?duiValue)
                }
            }
        """
    ] .

```

Full unmodified selected SHACL:
```turtle
@prefix gen: <urn:nltl:generated-shape:> .
@prefix nltl: <https://w3id.org/nltl/vocab#> .
@prefix sh: <http://www.w3.org/ns/shacl#> .
@prefix xsd: <http://www.w3.org/2001/XMLSchema#> .

gen:TRF001ShipShape
    a sh:NodeShape ;
    sh:targetClass nltl:ship ;
    sh:or (
        [
            sh:property [
                sh:path nltl:constructionContractDate ;
                sh:datatype xsd:date ;
                sh:minCount 1 ;
                sh:minInclusive "2021-07-05"^^xsd:date ;
                sh:message "A construction contract date on or after 2021-07-05 establishes applicability of the 2021 Ice Class Regulations."
            ]
        ]
        [
            sh:property [
                sh:path nltl:constructionContractDate ;
                sh:datatype xsd:date ;
                sh:minCount 1 ;
                sh:maxExclusive "2021-07-05"^^xsd:date ;
                sh:message "A construction contract date before 2021-07-05 establishes non-applicability of the 2021 Ice Class Regulations under this date rule."
            ]
        ]
    ) ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:constructionContractDate ;
        sh:datatype xsd:date ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:message "The construction contract date is required to determine which regulatory edition applies."
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:applicableIceClassRegulatoryProvision ;
        sh:nodeKind sh:IRI ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:hasValue nltl:iceClassSection1Point8Provision2021 ;
        sh:message "The 2021 section 1.8 Ice Classes provision applies to every ship."
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:applicableIceClassRegulatoryProvision ;
        sh:nodeKind sh:IRI ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:hasValue nltl:iceClassDraughtChapter2Provision2021 ;
        sh:message "The 2021 chapter 2 Ice Class Draught provision applies to every ship."
    ] ;
    sh:sparql [
        sh:message "Ships contracted for construction on or after 2021-07-05 shall identify the 2021 Ice Class Regulations as the applicable edition." ;
        sh:select """
            PREFIX nltl: <https://w3id.org/nltl/vocab#>
            PREFIX xsd: <http://www.w3.org/2001/XMLSchema#>
            SELECT $this
            WHERE {
                $this nltl:constructionContractDate ?date .
                FILTER (?date >= "2021-07-05"^^xsd:date)
                FILTER NOT EXISTS {
                    $this nltl:applicableIceClassRegulationEdition
                        nltl:iceClassRegulationEdition2021 .
                }
            }
        """
    ] .

```

Full unmodified selected SHACL:
```turtle
@prefix gen: <urn:nltl:generated-shape:> .
@prefix nltl: <https://w3id.org/nltl/vocab#> .
@prefix sh: <http://www.w3.org/ns/shacl#> .
@prefix xsd: <http://www.w3.org/2001/XMLSchema#> .

gen:TRF001ShipShape
    a sh:NodeShape ;
    sh:targetClass nltl:ship ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:constructionContractDate ;
        sh:datatype xsd:date ;
        sh:message "The construction contract date must be an xsd:date when supplied."
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:applicableIceClassRegulatoryProvision ;
        sh:nodeKind sh:IRI ;
        sh:class nltl:iceClassRegulatoryProvisionValue ;
        sh:in (
            nltl:iceClassSection1Point8Provision2021
            nltl:iceClassDraughtChapter2Provision2021
        ) ;
        sh:minCount 2 ;
        sh:maxCount 2 ;
        sh:message "Both the 2021 section 1.8 Ice Classes provision and chapter 2 Ice Class Draught provision must apply."
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:applicableIceClassRegulationEdition ;
        sh:nodeKind sh:IRI ;
        sh:class nltl:iceClassRegulationEditionValue
    ] ;
    sh:sparql [
        sh:message "The 2021 Ice Class Regulations must be identified for ships contracted for construction on or after 5 July 2021, and must not be identified for ships contracted before that date." ;
        sh:select """
            PREFIX nltl: <https://w3id.org/nltl/vocab#>
            PREFIX xsd: <http://www.w3.org/2001/XMLSchema#>

            SELECT $this
            WHERE {
                $this nltl:constructionContractDate ?date .
                FILTER (
                    (
                        ?date >= "2021-07-05"^^xsd:date
                        && NOT EXISTS {
                            $this nltl:applicableIceClassRegulationEdition
                                nltl:iceClassRegulationEdition2021 .
                        }
                    )
                    ||
                    (
                        ?date < "2021-07-05"^^xsd:date
                        && EXISTS {
                            $this nltl:applicableIceClassRegulationEdition
                                nltl:iceClassRegulationEdition2021 .
                        }
                    )
                )
            }
        """
    ] .

```

```turtle
    ] ;
```

```turtle
    a sh:NodeShape ;
```

```turtle
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:plannedOrActualLatitude ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:maxCount 1 ;
        sh:node [
            sh:class qudt:QuantityValue ;
            sh:property [
                sh:path qudt:numericValue ;
                sh:minCount 1 ;
                sh:maxCount 1 ;
                sh:datatype xsd:decimal
            ] ;

                sh:path qudt:unit ;

            ]

    ] ;

        nltlgen:IMO26_013_ApplicableBranch
        nltlgen:IMO26_013_NonApplicableBranch
    ) .

    a sh:NodeShape ;
    sh:sparql [
        sh:message "The applicable branch requires a latitude over 80 degrees." ;
        sh:select """
            PREFIX nltl: <https://w3id.org/nltl/vocab#>
            PREFIX qudt: <http://qudt.org/schema/qudt/>
            PREFIX xsd: <http://www.w3.org/2001/XMLSchema#>
            SELECT $this
            WHERE {
                $this nltl:plannedOrActualLatitude ?latitudeNode .
                ?latitudeNode qudt:numericValue ?latitude .
                FILTER (?latitude <= 80)
            }
        """
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:gnssCompassOrEquivalentCount ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:maxCount 1 ;
        sh:datatype xsd:integer ;
        sh:minInclusive 1 ;
        sh:message "At latitudes over 80 degrees, at least one GNSS compass or equivalent is required."
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:connectedToMainPower ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:class nltl:benchmarkEntity ;
        sh:message "At latitudes over 80 degrees, the GNSS compass or equivalent must be connected to the main power source."
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:connectedToEmergencyPower ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:class nltl:benchmarkEntity ;
        sh:message "At latitudes over 80 degrees, the GNSS compass or equivalent must be connected to the emergency power source."
    ] .
```

```turtle
@prefix gs: <urn:nltl:generated-shape:> .
@prefix nltl: <https://w3id.org/nltl/vocab#> .
@prefix qudt: <http://qudt.org/schema/qudt/> .
@prefix sh: <http://www.w3.org/ns/shacl#> .
@prefix unit: <http://qudt.org/vocab/unit/> .
@prefix xsd: <http://www.w3.org/2001/XMLSchema#> .

gs:IMO26_013_ShipShape
    a sh:NodeShape ;
    sh:targetClass nltl:ship ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:plannedOrActualLatitude ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:node [
            sh:class qudt:QuantityValue ;
            sh:property [
                sh:path qudt:numericValue ;
                sh:minCount 1 ;
                sh:maxCount 1 ;
                sh:datatype xsd:decimal
            ] ;
            sh:property [
                sh:path qudt:unit ;
                sh:minCount 1 ;
                sh:maxCount 1 ;
                sh:hasValue unit:DEG
            ]
        ]
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:gnssCompassOrEquivalentCount ;
        sh:datatype xsd:integer
    ] ;
    sh:sparql [
        a sh:SPARQLConstraint ;
        sh:message "For ships proceeding to a latitude over 80 degrees, at least one GNSS compass or equivalent must be present and connected to both the main and emergency sources of power." ;
        sh:select """
            PREFIX nltl: <https://w3id.org/nltl/vocab#>
            PREFIX qudt: <http://qudt.org/schema/qudt/>
            PREFIX unit: <http://qudt.org/vocab/unit/>

            SELECT $this
            WHERE {
                $this nltl:plannedOrActualLatitude ?latitudeQuantity .
                ?latitudeQuantity qudt:numericValue ?latitude ;
                    qudt:unit unit:DEG .
                FILTER (?latitude > 80)
                FILTER (
                    NOT EXISTS {
                        $this nltl:gnssCompassOrEquivalentCount ?compassCount .
                        FILTER (?compassCount >= 1)
                    }
                    ||
                    NOT EXISTS {
                        $this nltl:connectedToMainPower ?mainPowerConnection .
                    }
                    ||
                    NOT EXISTS {
                        $this nltl:connectedToEmergencyPower ?emergencyPowerConnection .
                    }
                )
            }
        """
    ] .

```

Original source text:
Recognizing the limitations arising from battery life, procedures shall be developed and implemented such that mandatory communication equipment for use in survival craft, including liferafts and rescue boats, are available for operation during the maximum expected time of rescue.
Normalized requirement:
battery_life_management_procedure_present = true AND procedure_implementation_status = active AND every mandatory survival-craft communication device shall remain available for maximum_expected_rescue_time_days.
Required inputs: Implemented procedure, device inventory, battery/availability assessment, and rescue time

```turtle
@prefix nltl: <https://w3id.org/nltl/vocab#> .
@prefix qudt: <http://qudt.org/schema/qudt/> .
@prefix sh: <http://www.w3.org/ns/shacl#> .
@prefix xsd: <http://www.w3.org/2001/XMLSchema#> .
@prefix gen: <urn:nltl:generated-shape:> .
@prefix unit: <http://qudt.org/vocab/unit/> .

gen:IMO_099_ShipShape
    a sh:NodeShape ;
    sh:targetClass nltl:ship ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:batteryLifeManagementProcedurePresent ;
        sh:datatype xsd:boolean ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:maxCount 1 ;
        sh:hasValue true ;
        sh:message "The ship shall have a battery life management procedure."
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:procedureImplementationStatus ;
        sh:datatype xsd:boolean ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:maxCount 1 ;
        sh:hasValue true ;
        sh:message "The battery life management procedure shall be implemented."
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:communicationDeviceAvailability ;
        sh:node [
            sh:class qudt:QuantityValue ;
            sh:property [
                sh:path qudt:numericValue ;
                sh:datatype xsd:decimal ;
                sh:minCount 1 ;
                sh:maxCount 1
            ] ;
            sh:property [
                sh:path qudt:unit ;
                sh:hasValue unit:DAY ;
                sh:minCount 1 ;
                sh:maxCount 1
            ]
        ] ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:message "Every mandatory survival-craft communication device, including devices on liferafts and rescue boats, shall have availability recorded in days."
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:maximumExpectedRescueTime ;
        sh:node [
            sh:property [
                sh:path qudt:numericValue ;
                sh:datatype xsd:decimal ;
                sh:minCount 1 ;
                sh:maxCount 1
            ] ;
            sh:property [
                sh:path qudt:unit ;
                sh:hasValue unit:DAY ;
                sh:minCount 1 ;
                sh:maxCount 1
            ]
        ] ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:maxCount 1 ;
        sh:message "The maximum expected rescue time shall be recorded in days."
    ] ;
    sh:sparql [
        a sh:SPARQLConstraint ;
        sh:message "Communication equipment availability shall be at least the maximum expected time of rescue." ;
        sh:select """
            PREFIX nltl: <https://w3id.org/nltl/vocab#>
            PREFIX qudt: <http://qudt.org/schema/qudt/>
            PREFIX sh: <http://www.w3.org/ns/shacl#>
            PREFIX xsd: <http://www.w3.org/2001/XMLSchema#>
            SELECT $this
            WHERE {
                $this nltl:communicationDeviceAvailability ?availabilityNode .
                ?availabilityNode qudt:numericValue ?availability .
                $this nltl:maximumExpectedRescueTime ?rescueNode .
                ?rescueNode qudt:numericValue ?rescueTime .
                FILTER (?availability < ?rescueTime)
            }
        """
    ] .
```

Full unmodified selected SHACL:
```turtle
@prefix nltl: <https://w3id.org/nltl/vocab#> .
@prefix qudt: <http://qudt.org/schema/qudt/> .
@prefix sh: <http://www.w3.org/ns/shacl#> .
@prefix xsd: <http://www.w3.org/2001/XMLSchema#> .

<urn:nltl:generated-shape:IMO-099-ShipShape> a sh:NodeShape ;
    sh:targetClass nltl:ship ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:batteryLifeManagementProcedurePresent ;
        sh:datatype xsd:boolean ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:maxCount 1 ;
        sh:hasValue true ;
        sh:message "The battery-life management procedure must be recorded as present."
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:procedureImplementationStatus ;
        sh:datatype xsd:boolean ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:maxCount 1 ;
        sh:hasValue true ;
        sh:message "The battery-life management procedure must be recorded as implemented and active."
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:communicationDeviceAvailability ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:maxCount 1 ;
        sh:node <urn:nltl:generated-shape:IMO-099-AvailabilityQuantityShape> ;
        sh:message "Communication device availability must be provided in days."
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:maximumExpectedRescueTime ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:maxCount 1 ;
        sh:node <urn:nltl:generated-shape:IMO-099-RescueTimeQuantityShape> ;
        sh:message "The maximum expected rescue time must be provided in days."
    ] ;
    sh:sparql [
        a sh:SPARQLConstraint ;
        sh:message "Communication device availability must be at least the maximum expected rescue time." ;
        sh:select """
            PREFIX nltl: <https://w3id.org/nltl/vocab#>
            PREFIX qudt: <http://qudt.org/schema/qudt/>
            PREFIX xsd: <http://www.w3.org/2001/XMLSchema#>

            SELECT $this
            WHERE {
                $this nltl:communicationDeviceAvailability ?availabilityQuantity .
                ?availabilityQuantity qudt:numericValue ?availability .
                ?availabilityQuantity qudt:unit <http://qudt.org/vocab/unit/DAY> .

                $this nltl:maximumExpectedRescueTime ?rescueQuantity .
                ?rescueQuantity qudt:numericValue ?rescueTime .
                ?rescueQuantity qudt:unit <http://qudt.org/vocab/unit/DAY> .

                FILTER(?availability < ?rescueTime)
            }
        """
    ] .

<urn:nltl:generated-shape:IMO-099-AvailabilityQuantityShape> a sh:NodeShape ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path qudt:numericValue ;
        sh:datatype xsd:decimal ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:maxCount 1
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path qudt:unit ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:maxCount 1 ;
        sh:hasValue <http://qudt.org/vocab/unit/DAY>
    ] .

<urn:nltl:generated-shape:IMO-099-RescueTimeQuantityShape> a sh:NodeShape ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path qudt:numericValue ;
        sh:datatype xsd:decimal ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:maxCount 1
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path qudt:unit ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:maxCount 1 ;
        sh:hasValue <http://qudt.org/vocab/unit/DAY>
    ] .

```

Original source text:
Means shall be provided to ensure that combustion air for internal combustion engines driving essential machinery is maintained at a temperature in compliance with the criteria provided by the engine manufacturer.
Normalized requirement:
For every internal combustion engine driving essential machinery, combustion_air_temperature_control_present = true AND combustion_air_temperature_degC shall be within the engine manufacturer criteria.
Required inputs: Essential-engine inventory, control arrangement, observed/design temperature, and manufacturer limits

Full unmodified RDF Turtle:
```turtle
@prefix ex: <https://w3id.org/nltl/benchmark/imo-batch-e/IMO-047-P04/> .
@prefix nltl: <https://w3id.org/nltl/vocab#> .
@prefix qudt: <http://qudt.org/schema/qudt/> .
@prefix unit: <http://qudt.org/vocab/unit/> .
@prefix xsd: <http://www.w3.org/2001/XMLSchema#> .

ex:ship a nltl:ship ;
    nltl:hasEssentialEngine ex:engine1,
        ex:engine2 .

ex:engine1 a nltl:essentialEngine ;
    nltl:combustionAirTemperature ex:engine1_combustionAirTemperatureValue ;
    nltl:combustionAirTemperatureControlPresent true ;
    nltl:manufacturerMaxAirTemperature ex:engine1_manufacturerMaxAirTemperatureValue ;
    nltl:manufacturerMinAirTemperature ex:engine1_manufacturerMinAirTemperatureValue .

ex:engine1_combustionAirTemperatureValue a qudt:QuantityValue ;
    qudt:numericValue 0.0 ;
    qudt:unit unit:DEG_C .

ex:engine1_manufacturerMaxAirTemperatureValue a qudt:QuantityValue ;
    qudt:numericValue 45.0 ;
    qudt:unit unit:DEG_C .

ex:engine1_manufacturerMinAirTemperatureValue a qudt:QuantityValue ;
    qudt:numericValue -20.0 ;
    qudt:unit unit:DEG_C .

ex:engine2 a nltl:essentialEngine ;
```

```turtle
@prefix gen: <urn:nltl:generated-shape:> .
@prefix nltl: <https://w3id.org/nltl/vocab#> .
@prefix qudt: <http://qudt.org/schema/qudt/> .
@prefix sh: <http://www.w3.org/ns/shacl#> .
@prefix xsd: <http://www.w3.org/2001/XMLSchema#> .

gen:IMO047ShipShape a sh:NodeShape ;
    sh:targetClass nltl:ship ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:hasEssentialEngine ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:class nltl:essentialEngine ;
        sh:message "The ship must identify at least one essential engine."
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:combustionAirTemperatureControlPresent ;
        sh:datatype xsd:boolean ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:maxCount 1 ;
        sh:hasValue true ;
        sh:message "Combustion-air temperature control must be recorded as present."
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:combustionAirTemperature ;
        sh:node gen:IMO047TemperatureQuantityShape ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:maxCount 1 ;
        sh:message "The combustion-air temperature must be supplied in degrees Celsius."
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:manufacturerMinAirTemperature ;
        sh:node gen:IMO047TemperatureQuantityShape ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:maxCount 1 ;
        sh:message "The manufacturer's minimum combustion-air temperature criterion must be supplied in degrees Celsius."
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:manufacturerMaxAirTemperature ;
        sh:node gen:IMO047TemperatureQuantityShape ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:maxCount 1 ;
        sh:message "The manufacturer's maximum combustion-air temperature criterion must be supplied in degrees Celsius."
    ] ;
    sh:sparql [
        a sh:SPARQLConstraint ;
        sh:message "The combustion-air temperature must be within the engine manufacturer's minimum and maximum criteria." ;
        sh:select """
            PREFIX nltl: <https://w3id.org/nltl/vocab#>
            PREFIX qudt: <http://qudt.org/schema/qudt/>
            PREFIX xsd: <http://www.w3.org/2001/XMLSchema#>

            SELECT $this
            WHERE {
                FILTER NOT EXISTS {
                    $this nltl:combustionAirTemperatureControlPresent true .
                    $this nltl:combustionAirTemperature ?temperatureQuantity .
                    $this nltl:manufacturerMinAirTemperature ?minimumQuantity .
                    $this nltl:manufacturerMaxAirTemperature ?maximumQuantity .

                    ?temperatureQuantity
                        qudt:numericValue ?temperatureValue ;
                        qudt:unit <http://qudt.org/vocab/unit/DEG_C> .

                    ?minimumQuantity
                        qudt:numericValue ?minimumValue ;
                        qudt:unit <http://qudt.org/vocab/unit/DEG_C> .

                    ?maximumQuantity
                        qudt:numericValue ?maximumValue ;
                        qudt:unit <http://qudt.org/vocab/unit/DEG_C> .

                    FILTER (
                        datatype(?temperatureValue) = xsd:decimal &&
                        datatype(?minimumValue) = xsd:decimal &&
                        datatype(?maximumValue) = xsd:decimal &&
                        ?temperatureValue >= ?minimumValue &&
                        ?temperatureValue <= ?maximumValue
                    )
                }
            }
        """
    ] .

gen:IMO047TemperatureQuantityShape a sh:NodeShape ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path qudt:numericValue ;
        sh:datatype xsd:decimal ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:maxCount 1
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path qudt:unit ;
        sh:hasValue <http://qudt.org/vocab/unit/DEG_C> ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:maxCount 1
    ] .

```

```turtle
@prefix nltl: <https://w3id.org/nltl/vocab#> .
@prefix nltlgen: <urn:nltl:generated-shape:> .
@prefix qudt: <http://qudt.org/schema/qudt/> .
@prefix sh: <http://www.w3.org/ns/shacl#> .
@prefix xsd: <http://www.w3.org/2001/XMLSchema#> .

nltlgen:IMO_047_TemperatureQuantityShape
    a sh:NodeShape ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path qudt:numericValue ;
        sh:datatype xsd:decimal ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:maxCount 1 ;
        sh:message "A combustion-air temperature quantity must have one decimal numeric value."
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path qudt:unit ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:maxCount 1 ;
        sh:hasValue <http://qudt.org/vocab/unit/DEG_C> ;
        sh:message "A combustion-air temperature quantity must use degrees Celsius."
    ] .

nltlgen:IMO_047_EssentialEngineShape
    a sh:NodeShape ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:combustionAirTemperatureControlPresent ;
        sh:datatype xsd:boolean ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:maxCount 1 ;
        sh:hasValue true ;
        sh:message "An essential engine must provide combustion-air temperature control."
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:combustionAirTemperature ;
        sh:node nltlgen:IMO_047_TemperatureQuantityShape ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:maxCount 1 ;
        sh:message "An essential engine must record its combustion-air temperature."
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:manufacturerMinAirTemperature ;
        sh:node nltlgen:IMO_047_TemperatureQuantityShape ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:maxCount 1 ;
        sh:message "An essential engine must provide its manufacturer minimum combustion-air temperature."
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:manufacturerMaxAirTemperature ;
        sh:node nltlgen:IMO_047_TemperatureQuantityShape ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:maxCount 1 ;
        sh:message "An essential engine must provide its manufacturer maximum combustion-air temperature."
    ] ;
    sh:sparql [
        a sh:SPARQLConstraint ;
        sh:message "Combustion-air temperature must be within the essential engine manufacturer's temperature limits." ;
        sh:select """
PREFIX nltl: <https://w3id.org/nltl/vocab#>
PREFIX xsd: <http://www.w3.org/2001/XMLSchema#>

SELECT $this
WHERE {
    $this nltl:combustionAirTemperature/qudt:numericValue ?temperature .
    $this nltl:manufacturerMinAirTemperature/qudt:numericValue ?minimum .
    $this nltl:manufacturerMaxAirTemperature/qudt:numericValue ?maximum .
    FILTER (?temperature < ?minimum || ?temperature > ?maximum)
}
"""
    ] .

nltlgen:IMO_047_ShipShape
    a sh:NodeShape ;
    sh:targetClass nltl:ship ;
```

Full unmodified selected SHACL:
```turtle
@prefix gen: <urn:nltl:generated-shape:> .
@prefix nltl: <https://w3id.org/nltl/vocab#> .
@prefix qudt: <http://qudt.org/schema/qudt/> .
@prefix sh: <http://www.w3.org/ns/shacl#> .
@prefix xsd: <http://www.w3.org/2001/XMLSchema#> .

gen:IMO_047_ShipShape
    a sh:NodeShape ;
    sh:targetClass nltl:ship ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:hasEssentialEngine ;
        sh:node [
            sh:class nltl:essentialEngine
        ] ;
        sh:message "Each linked essential engine must be represented as an essential engine."
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:combustionAirTemperatureControlPresent ;
        sh:datatype xsd:boolean ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:maxCount 1 ;
        sh:hasValue true ;
        sh:message "Combustion-air temperature control must be recorded as present."
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:combustionAirTemperature ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:maxCount 1 ;
        sh:node [
            sh:property [
                sh:path qudt:numericValue ;
                sh:datatype xsd:decimal ;
                sh:minCount 1 ;
                sh:maxCount 1
            ] ;
            sh:property [
                sh:path qudt:unit ;
                sh:minCount 1 ;
                sh:maxCount 1 ;
                sh:hasValue qudt:DEG_C
            ]
        ] ;
        sh:message "Combustion air temperature must be provided as a single degree-Celsius quantity."
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:manufacturerMinAirTemperature ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:maxCount 1 ;
        sh:node [
            sh:property [
                sh:path qudt:numericValue ;
                sh:datatype xsd:decimal ;
                sh:minCount 1 ;
                sh:maxCount 1
            ] ;
            sh:property [
                sh:path qudt:unit ;
                sh:minCount 1 ;
                sh:maxCount 1 ;
                sh:hasValue qudt:DEG_C
            ]
        ] ;
        sh:message "The manufacturer's minimum air temperature must be provided as a single degree-Celsius quantity."
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:manufacturerMaxAirTemperature ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:maxCount 1 ;
        sh:node [
            sh:property [
                sh:path qudt:numericValue ;
                sh:datatype xsd:decimal ;
                sh:minCount 1 ;
                sh:maxCount 1
            ] ;
            sh:property [
                sh:path qudt:unit ;
                sh:minCount 1 ;
                sh:maxCount 1 ;
                sh:hasValue qudt:DEG_C
            ]
        ] ;
        sh:message "The manufacturer's maximum air temperature must be provided as a single degree-Celsius quantity."
    ] ;
    sh:sparql [
        a sh:SPARQLConstraint ;
        sh:message "Combustion air temperature must be within the manufacturer's minimum and maximum air-temperature criteria." ;
        sh:select """
PREFIX nltl: <https://w3id.org/nltl/vocab#>
PREFIX qudt: <http://qudt.org/schema/qudt/>
PREFIX sh: <http://www.w3.org/ns/shacl#>
PREFIX xsd: <http://www.w3.org/2001/XMLSchema#>

SELECT $this
WHERE {
    $this nltl:combustionAirTemperature ?temperatureQuantity .
    ?temperatureQuantity qudt:numericValue ?temperatureValue .

    $this nltl:manufacturerMinAirTemperature ?minimumQuantity .
    ?minimumQuantity qudt:numericValue ?minimumValue .

    $this nltl:manufacturerMaxAirTemperature ?maximumQuantity .
    ?maximumQuantity qudt:numericValue ?maximumValue .

    FILTER (
        ?temperatureValue < ?minimumValue ||
        ?temperatureValue > ?maximumValue
    )
}
"""
    ] .

```

Seawater supplies for machinery systems shall be designed to prevent ingestion of ice, or otherwise arranged to ensure functionality.
Normalized requirement:
Every machinery seawater supply shall have ice_ingestion_prevention_present = true OR alternate_functionality_arrangement_approval_status = approved.
Required inputs: Seawater-supply design and alternative-arrangement approval evidence
